/**
 * LlmGateway implementation for Ollama local models.
 * Uses the Ollama /api/chat endpoint (supports tool calling since Ollama 0.3+).
 *
 * @param model e.g. "llama3.1", "mistral", "qwen2.5-coder"
 * @param baseUrl Ollama server URL (default: http://localhost:11434)
 */
class OllamaGateway(
    private val model: String = "llama3.1",
    private val baseUrl: String = "http://localhost:11434",
    private val timeoutSeconds: Long = 120,
) : LlmGateway {

    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OllamaGateway::class.java)
    private val http = HttpClient.newBuilder().connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).build()
    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }

    override fun complete(
        messages: List<LlmMessage>,
        tools: List<LlmTool>,
        systemPrompt: String?,
    ): LlmResponse {
        val allMessages = buildList {
            if (systemPrompt != null) add(LlmMessage(LlmMessage.Role.system, systemPrompt))
            addAll(messages)
        }

        val body = buildJsonObject {
            put("model", JsonPrimitive(model))
            put("stream", JsonPrimitive(false))
            put("messages", buildMessagesArray(allMessages))
            if (tools.isNotEmpty()) put("tools", buildToolsArray(tools))
        }

        log.debug("Ollama request: model={} messages={} tools={}", model, allMessages.size, tools.size)

        val request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("$baseUrl/api/chat"))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json.encodeToString(body)))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(timeoutSeconds))
            .build()

💭 #84 Reasoning 30m 43s
Good. Now I have a clear picture of the codebase. Let me create the plan and launch the implementation agent. Key design decisions for the implementation: 1. **Keep `LlmGateway` as-is** - it's used by AgentRunner, MemoryAugmentedGateway etc. Don't break it. 2. **New `LlmProvider` interface** - parallel abstraction with `suspend fun complete`, `Flow<CompletionChunk>` for streaming, `isLocal` flag. 3. **`CompletionRequest`/`CompletionResponse`** - new unified types that reference existing `LlmMessage`/`LlmTool`/`LlmToolCall` to avoid duplication 4. **`LlmProviderAdapter`** - bridges the new provider interface back to the existing gateway using blocking calls, so AgentRunner can work with any provider through this adapter. 5. **11 provider implementations** - one for each service in the `dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl/` package. 6. **`LlmProviderRegistry`** - a thread-safe registry object to manage providers, plus a `MultiProviderRouter` for fallback chains. 7. **Configuration layer** - YAML config file with a corresponding data class to load provider settings. 8. **Android UI updates** - modifying the settings screen and view model to let users manage providers, and adding the security crypto dependency to the build config. 9. **Provider tests** - unit tests for each implementation using mock HTTP servers to verify behavior. Now I'm ready to write out the full implementation plan and get the agent started.

private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MultiProviderRouter::class.java)\n\n    suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {\n        val order = if (fallbackOrder.isEmpty()) registry.all().map { it.id } else fallbackOrder\n        var lastError: Exception? = null\n        for (id in order) {\n            val provider = registry.get(id) ?: continue\n            try {\n                log.debug(\"Trying provider: {}\", id)\n                val resp = provider.complete(request)\n                MetricsCollector.inc(\"llm_provider_success\", mapOf(\"provider\" to id))\n                return resp\n            } catch (e: Exception) {\n                log.warn(\"Provider {} failed: {}\", id, e.message)\n                MetricsCollector.inc(\"llm_provider_fallback\", mapOf(\"from\" to id))\n                lastError = e\n            }\n        }\n        throw LlmException(\"All fallback dev.agentshell.llm.provider\n\nimport com.charleskorn.kaml.Yaml\nimport kotlinx.serialization.Serializable\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory\n\n@Serializable\ndata class ProviderYaml(\n    val id: String = \"\",\n    val enabled: Boolean = false,\n    val apiKey: String? = null,\n    val model: String? = null,\n    val host: String? = null,\n    val endpoint: String? = null,\n    val deployment: String? = null,\n    val projectId: String? = null,\n    val location: String? = null,\n    val modelPath: String? = null,\n    val binaryPath: String? = null,\n    val threads: Int = 4,\n    val maxTokens: Int = 4096,\n    val temperature: Double = 0.7,\n)\n\n@Serializable\ndata class LlmProvidersConfig(\n    val providers: List<ProviderYaml> = emptyList(),\n)\n\nobject LlmProvidersLoader {\n    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(LlmProvidersLoader::class.java)\n\n    fun load(resourcePath: String = \"llm_providers.yaml\"): LlmProvidersConfig {\n        val stream = Thread.currentThread().contextClassLoader\n            ?.getResourceAsStream(resourcePath)\n            ?: LlmProvidersLoader::class.java.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream(resourcePath)\n            ?: run {\n                log.warn(\"llm_providers.yaml not found, using empty config\")\n                return LlmProvidersConfig()\n            }\n        return try {\n            val text = stream.bufferedReader().readText()\n                .replace(Regex(\"\\\\$\\\\{([^}]+)\\\\}\")) { mr ->\n                    System.getenv(mr.groupValues[1]) ?: \"\"\n                }\n            Yaml.default.decodeFromString(LlmProvidersConfig.serializer(), text)\n        } catch (e: Exception) {\n            log.error(\"Failed to load llm_providers.yaml: {}\", e.message)\n            LlmProvidersConfig()\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun registerAll(config: LlmProvidersConfig) {\n        val okHttpClient = okhttp3.OkHttpClient.Builder()\n            .callTimeout(60, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)\n            .connectTimeout(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)\n            .build()\n\n        config.providers.filter { it.enabled }.forEach { p ->\n            val provider: LlmProvider? = when (p.id) {\n                \"openai\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.OpenAiProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"anthropic\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.AnthropicProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"azure_openai\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.AzureOpenAiProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"vertex_ai\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.VertexAiProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"ollama\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.OllamaProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"huggingface\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.HuggingFaceProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"cohere\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.CohereProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"mistral\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.MistralProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"groq\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.GroqProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"deepseek\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.DeepSeekProvider(okHttpClient, p)\n                \"llama_cpp\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.LlamaCppProvider(p)\n                else -> { log.warn(\"Unknown provider id: {}\", p.id); null }\n            }\n            provider?.let { LlmProviderRegistry.register(it) }\n        }\n        log.info(\"Registered {} LLM providers\", LlmProviderRegistry.all().size)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Step 7: Create all 11 provider implementations\n\n#### `agent-core/src/main/kotlin/dev/agentshell/llm/provider/impl/BaseHttpProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmTool\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmToolCall\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionChunk\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.LlmProvider\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.withContext\nimport kotlinx.serialization.json.*\nimport okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType\nimport okhttp3.OkHttpClient\nimport okhttp3.Request\nimport okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory\n\nabstract class BaseHttpProvider(protected val http: OkHttpClient) : LlmProvider {\n    protected val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(this::class.java)\n    protected val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }\n    protected val JSON_MT = \"application/json\".toMediaType()\n\n    protected suspend fun post(url: String, body: JsonObject, headers: Map<String, String>): String =\n        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {\n            val reqBody = json.encodeToString(JsonObject.serializer(), body).toRequestBody(JSON_MT)\n            val reqBuilder = Request.Builder().url(url).post(reqBody)\n            headers.forEach { (k, v) -> reqBuilder.header(k, v) }\n            http.newCall(reqBuilder.build()).execute().use { resp ->\n                val bodyStr = resp.body?.string() ?: \"\"\n                if (!resp.isSuccessful) {\n                    log.error(\"{} HTTP {}: {}\", id, resp.code, bodyStr.take(300))\n                    throw dev.agentshell.llm.provider.LlmException(\"$id HTTP ${resp.code}: ${bodyStr.take(200)}\")\n                }\n                bodyStr\n            }\n        }\n\n    /** Build messages array in OpenAI format */\n    protected fun buildMessagesArray(messages: List<LlmMessage>, systemPrompt: String?): JsonArray = buildJsonArray {\n        systemPrompt?.let { add(buildJsonObject { put(\"role\", \"system\"); put(\"content\", it) }) }\n        for (msg in messages) {\n            add(buildJsonObject {\n                put(\"role\", msg.role.name)\n                msg.toolCallId?.let { put(\"tool_call_id\", it) }\n                msg.content?.let { put(\"content\", it) }\n                if (msg.toolCalls.isNotEmpty()) {\n                    put(\"tool_calls\", buildJsonArray {\n                        msg.toolCalls.forEach { tc ->\n                            add(buildJsonObject {\n                                put(\"id\", tc.id)\n                                put(\"type\", \"function\")\n                                put(\"function\", buildJsonObject {\n                                    put(\"name\", tc.toolName)\n                                    put(\"arguments\", tc.argumentsJson)\n                                })\n                            })\n                        }\n                    })\n                }\n            })\n        }\n    }\n\n    protected fun buildToolsArray(tools: List<LlmTool>): JsonArray = buildJsonArray {\n        tools.forEach { tool ->\n            add(buildJsonObject {\n                put(\"type\", \"function\")\n                put(\"function\", buildJsonObject {\n                    put(\"name\", tool.name)\n                    put(\"description\", tool.description)\n                    put(\"parameters\", json.parseToJsonElement(tool.parametersJson))\n                })\n            })\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Parse OpenAI-compatible response */\n    protected fun parseOpenAiResponse(body: String, provId: String, modelName: String): CompletionResponse {\n        val root = json.parseToJsonElement(body).jsonObject\n        val usage = root[\"usage\"]?.jsonObject\n        val choice = root[\"choices\"]?.jsonArray?.firstOrNull()?.jsonObject\n            ?: return CompletionResponse(content = null, providerId = provId, modelUsed = modelName)\n        val message = choice[\"message\"]?.jsonObject\n            ?: return CompletionResponse(content = null, providerId = provId, modelUsed = modelName)\n        val content = message[\"content\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.contentOrNull\n        val finishReason = choice[\"finish_reason\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.contentOrNull\n        val toolCalls = message[\"tool_calls\"]?.jsonArray?.map { tc ->\n            val o = tc.jsonObject\n            val fn = o[\"function\"]?.jsonObject\n            LlmToolCall(\n                id = o[\"id\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content ?: \"\",\n                toolName = fn?.get(\"name\")?.jsonPrimitive?.content ?: \"\",\n                argumentsJson = fn?.get(\"arguments\")?.jsonPrimitive?.content ?: \"{}\",\n            )\n        } ?: emptyList()\n        val stopReason = when {\n            toolCalls.isNotEmpty() -> dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse.StopReason.TOOL_USE\n            finishReason == \"length\" -> dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse.StopReason.MAX_TOKENS\n            else -> dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse.StopReason.END_TURN\n        }\n        return CompletionResponse(\n            content = content,\n            toolCalls = toolCalls,\n            stopReason = stopReason,\n            inputTokens = usage?.get(\"prompt_tokens\")?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0,\n            outputTokens = usage?.get(\"completion_tokens\")?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0,\n            providerId = provId,\n            modelUsed = modelName,\n        )\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/OpenAiProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.ProviderYaml\nimport kotlinx.serialization.json.*\nimport okhttp3.OkHttpClient\n\nclass OpenAiProvider(http: OkHttpClient, private val cfg: ProviderYaml) : BaseHttpProvider(http) {\n    override val id = \"openai\"\n    override val displayName = \"OpenAI\"\n    private val apiKey get() = cfg.apiKey?.ifBlank { null } ?: System.getenv(\"OPENAI_API_KEY\") ?: \"\"\n    private val model get() = cfg.model ?: \"gpt-4o-mini\"\n    private val baseUrl get() = cfg.endpoint?.trimEnd('/') ?: \"https://api.openai.com/v1\"\n\n    override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {\n        val body = buildJsonObject {\n            put(\"model\", request.modelOverride ?: model)\n            put(\"max_tokens\", request.maxTokens)\n            put(\"temperature\", request.temperature)\n            put(\"messages\", buildMessagesArray(request.messages, request.systemPrompt))\n            if (request.tools.isNotEmpty()) put(\"tools\", buildToolsArray(request.tools))\n        }\n        val resp = post(\"$baseUrl/chat/completions\", body, mapOf(\"Authorization\" to \"Bearer $apiKey\"))\n        return parseOpenAiResponse(resp, id, model)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/AnthropicProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmToolCall\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.ProviderYaml\nimport kotlinx.serialization.json.*\nimport okhttp3.OkHttpClient\n\nclass AnthropicProvider(http: OkHttpClient, private val cfg: ProviderYaml) : BaseHttpProvider(http) {\n    override val id = \"anthropic\"\n    override val displayName = \"Anthropic (Claude)\"\n    private val apiKey get() = cfg.apiKey?.ifBlank { null } ?: System.getenv(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") ?: \"\"\n    private val model get() = cfg.model ?: \"claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022\"\n\n    override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {\n        // Anthropic separates system prompt from messages\n        val userMessages = request.messages.filter { it.role != LlmMessage.Role.system }\n        val sysPrompt = request.systemPrompt\n            ?: request.messages.firstOrNull { it.role == LlmMessage.Role.system }?.content\n\n        val body = buildJsonObject {\n            put(\"model\", request.modelOverride ?: model)\n            put(\"max_tokens\", request.maxTokens)\n            sysPrompt?.let { put(\"system\", it) }\n            put(\"messages\", buildJsonArray {\n                userMessages.forEach { msg ->\n                    add(buildJsonObject {\n                        put(\"role\", if (msg.role == LlmMessage.Role.assistant) \"assistant\" else \"user\")\n                        put(\"content\", msg.content ?: \"\")\n                    })\n                }\n            })\n        }\n        val resp = post(\n            \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\", body, mapOf(\n                \"x-api-key\" to apiKey,\n                \"anthropic-version\" to \"2023-06-01\",\n            )\n        )\n        return parseAnthropicResponse(resp)\n    }\n\n    private fun parseAnthropicResponse(body: String): CompletionResponse {\n        val root = json.parseToJsonElement(body).jsonObject\n        val content = root[\"content\"]?.jsonArray\n            ?.firstOrNull { it.jsonObject[\"type\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content == \"text\" }\n            ?.jsonObject?.get(\"text\")?.jsonPrimitive?.content\n        val usage = root[\"usage\"]?.jsonObject\n        val stopReason = when (root[\"stop_reason\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content) {\n            \"end_turn\" -> LlmResponse.StopReason.END_TURN\n            \"max_tokens\" -> LlmResponse.StopReason.MAX_TOKENS\n            else -> LlmResponse.StopReason.END_TURN\n        }\n        return CompletionResponse(\n            content = content,\n            stopReason = stopReason,\n            inputTokens = usage?.get(\"input_tokens\")?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0,\n            outputTokens = usage?.get(\"output_tokens\")?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0,\n            providerId = id,\n            modelUsed = model,\n        )\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/AzureOpenAiProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.ProviderYaml\nimport kotlinx.serialization.json.*\nimport okhttp3.OkHttpClient\n\n/** Vertex AI via Gemini REST endpoint (no Google Auth library — uses API key or access token from env) */\nclass VertexAiProvider(http: OkHttpClient, private val cfg: ProviderYaml) : BaseHttpProvider(http) {\n    override val id = \"vertex_ai\"\n    override val displayName = \"Vertex AI (Gemini)\"\n    private val project get() = cfg.projectId ?: System.getenv(\"GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT\") ?: \"\"\n    private val location get() = cfg.location ?: \"us-central1\"\n    private val model get() = cfg.model ?: \"gemini-1.5-flash\"\n    private val accessToken get() = System.getenv(\"GOOGLE_ACCESS_TOKEN\") ?: \"\"\n    private val url get() = \"https://$location-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/$project/locations/$location/publishers/google/models/$model:generateContent\"\n\n    override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {\n        val contents = buildJsonArray {\n            request.messages.filter { it.role != LlmMessage.Role.system }.forEach { msg ->\n                add(buildJsonObject {\n                    put(\"role\", if (msg.role == LlmMessage.Role.assistant) \"model\" else \"user\")\n                    put(\"parts\", buildJsonArray { add(buildJsonObject { put(\"text\", msg.content ?: \"\") }) })\n                })\n            }\n        }\n        val body = buildJsonObject {\n            put(\"contents\", contents)\n            request.systemPrompt?.let {\n                put(\"systemInstruction\", buildJsonObject {\n                    put(\"parts\", buildJsonArray { add(buildJsonObject { put(\"text\", it) }) })\n                })\n            }\n        }\n        val resp = post(url, body, mapOf(\"Authorization\" to \"Bearer $accessToken\"))\n        return parseVertexResponse(resp)\n    }\n\n    private fun parseVertexResponse(body: String): CompletionResponse {\n        val root = json.parseToJsonElement(body).jsonObject\n        val candidate = root[\"candidates\"]?.jsonArray?.firstOrNull()?.jsonObject\n        val content = candidate?.get(\"content\")?.jsonObject\n            ?.get(\"parts\")?.jsonArray?.firstOrNull()?.jsonObject\n            ?.get(\"text\")?.jsonPrimitive?.content\n        return CompletionResponse(content = content, providerId = id, modelUsed = model)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/OllamaProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmToolCall\nimport (Local)\"\n    override val isLocal = true\n    private val model get() = cfg.model ?: \"llama3.2\"\n    private val baseUrl get() = cfg.host?.trimEnd('/') ?: \"http://localhost:11434\"\n\n })\n            put(\"messages\", buildMessagesArray(request.messages, request.systemPrompt))\n            if (request.tools.isNotEmpty()) put(\"tools\", buildToolsArray(request.tools))\n        }\n        val resp = post(\"$baseUrl/api/chat\", body, emptyMap())\n        return parseOllamaResponse(resp)\n    }\n\n    private fun parseOllamaResponse(body: String): CompletionResponse {\n        val json.encodeToString(JsonElement.serializer(), it) } ?: \"{}\",\n            )\n        } ?: emptyList()\n        val done = root[\"done\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.booleanOrNull ?: true\n        val prompt = root[\"prompt_eval_count\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0\n        val eval = root[\"eval_count\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0\n        return CompletionResponse(\n            content = content,\n            toolCalls = toolCalls,\n            stopReason = if (toolCalls.isNotEmpty()) LlmResponse.StopReason.TOOL_USE else LlmResponse.StopReason.END_TURN,\n            inputTokens = prompt,\n            outputTokens = eval,\n            providerId = id,\n            modelUsed = model,\n        )\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/HuggingFaceProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport { null } ?: System.getenv(\"COHERE_API_KEY\") ?: \"\"\n    private val model get() = cfg.model ?: \"command-r-plus\"\n\n    override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {\n        // Cohere v2 API is OpenAI-compatible\n        val body = buildJsonObject {\n            put(\"model\", request.modelOverride ?: model)\n            put(\"max_tokens\", CompletionResponse {\n        // Cohere v2 returns OpenAI-compatible format\n        return try {\n            parseOpenAiResponse(body, id, model)\n        } catch (e: Exception) {\n            // Fallback: legacy Cohere format\n            val root = json.parseToJsonElement(body).jsonObject\n            val text = root[\"text\"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content\n            CompletionResponse(content = text, providerId = id, modelUsed = model)\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/MistralProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.ProviderYaml\nimport kotlinx.serialization.json.*\nimport \"DeepSeek\"\n    private val apiKey get() = cfg.apiKey?.ifBlank { null } ?: System.getenv(\"DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\") ?: \"\"\n    private val model get() = cfg.model ?: \"deepseek-chat\"\n\n    override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {\n        val body = buildJsonObject {\n            put(\"model\", request.modelOverride ?: model)\n            put(\"max_tokens\", request.maxTokens)\n            put(\"temperature\", request.temperature)\n            put(\"messages\", buildMessagesArray(request.messages, request.systemPrompt))\n        }\n        val resp = post(\"https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions\", body, mapOf(\"Authorization\" to \"Bearer $apiKey\"))\n        return parseOpenAiResponse(resp, id, model)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### `impl/LlamaCppProvider.kt`\n```kotlin\npackage dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.LlmException\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.LlmProvider\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.provider.ProviderYaml\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.withContext\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory\nimport java.io.File\nimport java.util.UUID\n\nclass LlamaCppProvider(private val cfg: ProviderYaml) : LlmProvider {\n    override val id = \"llama_cpp\"\n    override val displayName = \"Llama.cpp (Local)\"\n    override val isLocal = true\n    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(LlamaCppProvider::class.java)\n    private val binaryPath get() = cfg.binaryPath ?: \"llama-cli\"\n    private val modelPath get() = cfg.modelPath ?: \"\"\n\n    override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {\n        if (modelPath.isBlank()) throw LlmException(\"llama_cpp: modelPath not configured\")\n        // Build a simple prompt from messages\n        val prompt = request.messages.joinToString(\"\\n\") { msg ->\n            val prefix = when (msg.role) {\n                LlmMessage.Role.system -> \"[SYSTEM]\"\n                LlmMessage.Role.user -> \"[USER]\"\n                LlmMessage.Role.assistant -> \"[ASSISTANT]\"\n                LlmMessage.Role.tool -> \"[TOOL]\"\n            }\n            \"$prefix ${msg.content ?: \"\"}\"\n        } + \"\\n[ASSISTANT]\"\n\n        val cmd = mutableListOf(\n            binaryPath,\n            \"-m\", modelPath,\n            \"--prompt\", prompt,\n            \"--temp\", request.temperature.toString(),\n            \"-n\", request.maxTokens.toString(),\n            \"-t\", cfg.threads.toString(),\n            \"--no-display-prompt\",\n        )\n        request.systemPrompt?.let { cmd.addAll(listOf(\"--system-prompt\", it)) }\n\n        log.debug(\"llama.cpp cmd: {} -m {} ...\", binaryPath, File(modelPath).name)\n        val proc = ProcessBuilder(cmd).redirectErrorStream(true).start()\n        val output = proc.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText()\n        val exit = proc.waitFor()\n        if (exit != 0) throw LlmException(\"llama-cpp exited $exit: ${output.take(200)}\")\n\n        CompletionResponse(\n            content = output.trim(),\n            providerId = id,\n            modelUsed = File(modelPath).name,\n        )\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Step 8: Create `agent-core/src/main/resources/llm_providers.yaml`\n\n```yaml\nproviders:\n  - id: openai\n    enabled: false\n    apiKey: dev.agentshell.llm.provider\n\nimport dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test\n\nclass MultiProviderRouterTest {\n\n    @AfterEach fun cleanup() = LlmProviderRegistry.clear()\n\n    private val req = CompletionRequest(messages = listOf(LlmMessage(LlmMessage.Role.user, \"hi\")))\n\n    @Test fun `returns first successful provider`() = runBlocking {\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(successProvider(\"a\", \"from-a\"))\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(successProvider(\"b\", \"from-b\"))\n        val router = MultiProviderRouter(fallbackOrder = listOf(\"a\", \"b\"))\n        val resp = router.complete(req)\n        assertEquals(\"from-a\", resp.content)\n    }\n\n    @Test fun `falls back on error`() = runBlocking {\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(failingProvider(\"x\"))\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(successProvider(\"y\", \"from-y\"))\n        val router = MultiProviderRouter(fallbackOrder = listOf(\"x\", \"y\"))\n        val resp = router.complete(req)\n        assertEquals(\"from-y\", resp.content)\n    }\n\n    @Test fun `throws when all fail`() {\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(failingProvider(\"p1\"))\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(failingProvider(\"p2\"))\n        val router = MultiProviderRouter(fallbackOrder = listOf(\"p1\", \"p2\"))\n        assertThrows(LlmException::class.java) { runBlocking { router.complete(req) } }\n    }\n\n    @Test fun `skips unregistered provider ids`() = runBlocking {\n        LlmProviderRegistry.register(successProvider(\"real\", \"ok\"))\n        val router = MultiProviderRouter(fallbackOrder = listOf(\"ghost\", \"real\"))\n        val resp = router.complete(req)\n        assertEquals(\"ok\", resp.content)\n    }\n\n    private fun successProvider(id: String, content: String) = object : LlmProvider {\n        override val id = id\n        override val displayName = id\n        override suspend fun complete(r: CompletionRequest) = CompletionResponse(content = content, providerId = id, modelUsed = \"m\")\n    }\n\n    private fun failingProvider(id: String) = object : LlmProvider {\n        override val id = id\n        override val displayName = id\n        override suspend fun complete(r: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse = throw LlmException(\"$id failed\")\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### 23. ## 2️⃣ Общая архитектура нового кода  
24. 
25. ```
26. agent-core
27.  └─ llm
28.      ├─ api          // Retrofit‑интерфейсы (OpenAiApi, AnthropicApi, …)
29.      ├─ model        // DTO‑классы (Request/Response) – один набор в `common`
30.      ├─ provider
31.      │    ├─ LLMProvider               // общ. интерфейс (как сейчас)
32.      │    ├─ OpenAiProvider
33.      │    ├─ AnthropicProvider
34.      │    ├─ AzureOpenAiProvider
35.      │    ├─ VertexAiProvider
36.      │    ├─ CohereProvider
37.      │    ├─ MistralProvider
38.      │    ├─ GroqProvider
39.      │    ├─ DeepSeekProvider
40.      │    ├─ HuggingFaceProvider
41.      │    ├─ OllamaProvider
42.      │    └─ LlamaCppProvider
43.      └─ config
44.           └─ LlmProvidersConfig.yaml   // расширенный список (см. ниже)
45. ```
46. 
47. *Все провайдеры **наследуют** единственный интерфейс `LLMProvider` (см. `agent‑core/src/main/kotlin/ru/agentshell/llm/provider/LLMProvider.kt`).*  
48. 
49. ### 2.1 Интерфейс (добавим потоковый метод)
50. 
51. ```kotlin
52. interface LLMProvider {
53.     val id: String                     // уникальный ключ, используется в config
54.     val displayName: String            // читаемое название в UI
55.     val isLocal: Boolean get() = false // переопределяется для Ollama / LlamaCpp
56. 
57.     /** Полный запрос → единственный ответ */
58.     suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse
59. 
60.     /** Потоковый вывод (SSE / Unix pipe). По‑умолчанию пустой поток. */
61.     fun stream(request: CompletionRequest): Flow<CompletionChunk> = emptyFlow()
62. }
63. ```
64. 
65. ### 2.2 Регистрация провайдера  
66. 
67. ```kotlin
68. object LlmProviderRegistry {
69.     private val map = mutableMapOf<String, LLMProvider>()
70.     fun register(p: LLMProvider) = map.put(p.id, p)
71.     fun get(id: String): LLMProvider? = map[id]
72.     fun all(): List<LLMProvider> = map.values.toList()
73. }
74. ```
75. 
76. *Регистрация происходит в Hilt‑модуле `ProviderModule` (см. ниже).*
77. 
78. ---
79. 
80. ## 3️⃣ Новые Kotlin‑классы (по‑провайдерно)
81. 
82. > **Все файлы находятся в** `agent-core/src/main/kotlin/ru/agentshell/llm/provider/`.  
83. > Ниже – шаблоны, которые можно скопировать и подставить нужные URL/Headers.
84. 
85. ### 3.1 AnthropicProvider
86. 
87. ```kotlin
88. class AnthropicProvider @Inject constructor(
89.     private val client: OkHttpClient,
90.     @Assisted private val cfg: AnthropicConfig   // apiKey, model, maxTokens, temperature
91. ) : LLMProvider {
92. 
93.     override val id = "anthropic"
94.     override val displayName = "Anthropic (Claude)"
95. 
96.     private val baseUrl = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1"
97. 
98.     override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {
99.         val payload = mapOf(
100.             "model" to cfg.model,
101.             "max_tokens" to cfg.maxTokens,
102.             "temperature" to cfg.temperature,
103.             "messages" to request.messages.map { mapOf("role" to it.role, "content" to it.content) }
104.         )
105.         val body = Moshi.Builder().build()
106.             .adapter(Map::class.java).toJson(payload)
107.             .toRequestBody("application/json".toMediaType())
108. 
109.         val httpReq = Request.Builder()
110.             .url("$baseUrl/messages")
111.             .addHeader("x-api-key", cfg.apiKey)
112.             .addHeader("anthropic-version", "2023-06-01")
113.             .post(body)
114.             .build()
115. 
116.         client.newCall(httpReq).execute().use { resp ->
117.             if (!resp.isSuccessful) throw LlmException("Anthropic error ${resp.code}")
118.             val json = resp.body!!.string()
119.             val dto = Moshi.Builder().build()
120.                 .adapter(AnthropicResponse::class.java).fromJson(json)!!
121. 
122.             return CompletionResponse(
123.                 id = dto.id,
124.                 model = cfg.model,
125.                 content = dto.content?.firstOrNull()?.text ?: "",
126.                 usage = dto.usage?.let { Usage(it.inputTokens, it.outputTokens, it.totalTokens) }
127.             )
128.         }
129.     }
130. 
131.     // stream() – реализуется через SSE, но пока не обязателен
132. }
133. ```
134. 
135. > **DTO‑классы** (`AnthropicResponse`, `AnthropicUsage`) помещаем в пакет `model`.
136.

359. class HuggingFaceProvider @Inject constructor(

362. ) : LLMProvider {
363. 
364.     override val id = "huggingface"
365.     override val displayName = "HuggingFace Inference"
366. 
367.     private val baseUrl = "https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/${cfg.model}"
368. 
369.     override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {
370.         val payload = mapOf(
371.             "inputs" to request.messages.lastOrNull()?.content.orEmpty(),
372.             "parameters" to mapOf(
373.                 "max_new_tokens" to cfg.maxTokens,
374.                 "temperature" to cfg.temperature
375.             )
376.         )
377.         val body = Moshi.Builder().build()
378.             .adapter(Map::class.java).toJson(payload)
379.             .toRequestBody("application/json".toMediaType())

384.             .post(body)
385.             .build()

388.             if (!resp.isSuccessful) throw LlmException("HF ${resp.code}")

390.             // HF возвращает массив строк → берём первую
391.             val list = Moshi.Builder().build()
392.                 .adapter<List<String>>(Types.newParameterizedType(List::class.java, String::class.java))
393.                 .fromJson(json) ?: emptyList()
394.             return CompletionResponse(
395.                 id = UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
396.                 model = cfg.model,
397.                 content = list.joinToString("\n"),
398.                 usage = null
399.             )
400.         }
401.     }
402. }

430. 
431. ```kotlin
432. class LlamaCppProvider @Inject constructor(
433.     @Assisted private val cfg: LlamaCppConfig,
434.     private val dispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher = Dispatchers.IO
435. ) : LLMProvider {
436. 
437.     override val id = "llama_cpp"
438.     override val displayName = "Llama.cpp"
439.     override val isLocal = true
440. 
441.     private val binary = File(cfg.binaryPath)   // будет скопировано в filesDir
442. 
443.     override suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse =
444.         withContext(dispatcher) {
445.             val proc = ProcessBuilder(
446.                 binary.absolutePath,
447.                 "-m", cfg.modelPath,
448.                 "-p", request.messages.joinToString("\n") { it.content },
449.                 "--temp", request.temperature.toString(),
450.                 "--max-tokens", request.maxTokens.toString(),
✔ #102 view - view the file at /root/.copilot/session-state/16e6a492-c3f7-471d-a6b4-bf2ec8942c72/files/paste-1775408298427.txt. 38m 6s
/root/.copilot/session-state/16e6a492-c3f7-471d-a6b4-bf2ec8942c72/files/paste-1775408298427.txt (lines 450-786)
450.                 "--max-tokens", request.maxTokens.toString(),
451.                 "--n-keep", "0"
452.             ).redirectErrorStream(true).start()
453. 
454.             val out = proc.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText()
455.             proc.waitFor()
456.             CompletionResponse(
457.                 id = UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
458.                 model = cfg.modelPath,
459.                 content = out.trim(),
460.                 usage = null
461.             )
462.         }
463. }
464. ```

569. 
570. ## 5️⃣ DI‑модуль (`ProviderModule.kt`)
571. 
572. ```kotlin
573. @Module
574. @InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
575. object ProviderModule {
576. 
577.     @Provides @Singleton
578.     fun provideOkHttpClient(): OkHttpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
579.         .callTimeout(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
580.         .build()
581. 
582.     @Provides @Singleton
583.     fun provideLlmConfig(@ApplicationContext ctx: Context): LlmProvidersConfig =
584.         ctx.assets.open("llm_providers.yaml")
585.             .bufferedReader().use { yaml ->
586.                 Moshi.Builder()
587.                     .add(KotlinJsonAdapterFactory())
588.                     .build()
589.                     .adapter(LlmProvidersConfig::class.java)
590.                     .fromJson(yaml.readText())!!
591.             }
592. 
593.     @Provides @Singleton
594.     fun registerAllProviders(
595.         client: OkHttpClient,
596.         ctx: Context,
597.         cfg: LlmProvidersConfig
598.     ): LlmProviderRegistry {
599. 
600.         cfg.providers.filter { it.enabled }.forEach { p ->
601.             when (p.id) {
602.                 "openai"      -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(OpenAiProvider(client, p.toOpenAiCfg()))
603.                 "anthropic"   -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(AnthropicProvider(client, p.toAnthropicCfg()))
604.                 "azure_openai"-> LlmProviderRegistry.register(AzureOpenAiProvider(client, p.toAzureCfg()))
605.                 "vertex_ai"   -> {
606.                     val cred = GoogleCredentials.fromStream(ctx.assets.open("service-account.json"))
607.                         .createScoped(listOf("https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform"))
608.                     LlmProviderRegistry.register(VertexAiProvider(cred, p.toVertexCfg()))
609.                 }
610.                 "cohere"      -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(CohereProvider(client, p.toCohereCfg()))
611.                 "mistral"     -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(MistralProvider(client, p.toMistralCfg()))
612.                 "groq"        -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(GroqProvider(client, p.toGroqCfg()))
613.                 "deepseek"    -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(DeepSeekProvider(client, p.toDeepSeekCfg()))
614.                 "huggingface" -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(HuggingFaceProvider(client, p.toHfCfg()))
615.                 "ollama"      -> LlmProviderRegistry.register(OllamaProvider(client, p.toOllamaCfg()))
616.                 "llama_cpp"   -> {
617.                     // копируем binary в filesDir, если ещё нет
618.                     val bin = copyAssetIfMissing(ctx, "llama-cli", "bin/llama-cli")
619.                     LlmProviderRegistry.register(
620.                         LlamaCppProvider(p.toLlamaCppCfg().copy(binaryPath = bin.absolutePath))
621.                     )
622.                 }
623.                 // открытые провайдеры уже есть…
624.                 else -> Unit
625.             }
626.         }
627. 
628.         // Dynamic JAR/Dex‑plugins
629.         PluginLoader.loadAll(ctx, "plugins") { jar ->
630.             val dyn = jar.loadClass("com.agent.plugin.DynamicProvider")
631.                 .getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance() as LLMProvider
632.             LlmProviderRegistry.register(dyn)
633.         }
634. 
635.         return LlmProviderRegistry
636.     }
637. 
638.     /** helper – копирует asset в filesDir, если его там ещё нет */
639.     private fun copyAssetIfMissing(ctx: Context, assetPath: String, targetSubDir: String): File {
640.         val target = File(ctx.filesDir, "$targetSubDir/$assetPath")
641.         if (!target.exists()) {
642.             target.parentFile?.mkdirs()
643.             ctx.assets.open(assetPath).use { `in` ->
644.                 target.outputStream().use { out -> `in`.copyTo(out) }
645.             }
646.             target.setExecutable(true)
647.         }
648.         return target
649.     }
650. }
651. ```
652. 
653. > **Конвертеры** (`toOpenAiCfg()`, `toAnthropicCfg()`, …) – простые extension‑функции, генерирующие data‑классы для каждого провайдера (в пакете `config`).  
654. 
655. ---
656. 
657. ## 6️⃣ UI‑изменения (Compose)
658. 
659. 1. **Settings → LLM Providers**  
660.    ```kotlin
661.    @Composable
662.    fun ProvidersScreen(viewModel: SettingsViewModel = hiltViewModel()) {
663.        val providers = viewModel.providers.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
664.        LazyColumn {
665.            items(providers.value) { prov ->
666.                ProviderItem(
667.                    provider = prov,
668.                    onToggle = { viewModel.toggleEnabled(prov.id, it) },
669.                    onEdit = { viewModel.openEditDialog(prov.id) }
670.                )
671.            }
672.        }
673.    }
674.    ```
675. 
676. 2. **Edit Dialog** – генерирует форму в зависимости от `provider.id` (например, у Anthropic нужен только `apiKey` и `model`). Поля сохраняются в `SharedPreferences` (encrypted) и сразу пишутся в `llm_providers.yaml` (перезаписывается в `filesDir`).
677. 
678. 3. **Run / Chat screen** – dropdown `DropdownMenu` со всеми `LlmProviderRegistry.all()`; выбранный сохраняется в `RunSettings` (Room) и передаётся в `AgentRunner` через `AgentRunConfig`.
679. 
680. 4. **Отображение локального провайдера** – иконка «🖥️» рядом с именем, подсказка “Works offline”.
681. 
682. ---
683. 
684. ## 7️⃣ Внедрение в `AgentRunner` (core)
685. 
686. ```kotlin
687. class AgentRunner @Inject constructor(
688.     private val registry: LlmProviderRegistry,
689.     private val configProvider: RunConfigProvider
690. ) {
691.     suspend fun run(runId: String) {
692.         val cfg = configProvider.getConfig(runId)
693.         val provider = registry.get(cfg.providerId) ?: throw IllegalStateException("Provider ${cfg.providerId} not found")
694.         val gateway = MemoryAugmentedGateway(provider, memoryStore)   // уже есть
695. 
696.         // дальше — прежний цикл, только gateway теперь может быть любой из новых
697.     }
698. }
699. ```
700. 
701. *`RunConfig.providerId` хранится в Room‑таблице `run_table` (добавлено поле `provider_id`). Миграция будет простой `ALTER TABLE run_table ADD COLUMN provider_id TEXT DEFAULT 'ollama'`.*
702. 
703. ---
704. 
705. ## 8️⃣ Тесты
706. 
707. | Тест‑пакет | Что покрывается |
708. |------------|-----------------|
709. | `OpenAiProviderTest` | уже есть |
710. | `AnthropicProviderTest` | мок‑сервер (MockWebServer) → проверка заголовков `x-api-key` и тела |
711. | `AzureOpenAiProviderTest` | проверка URL‑формата и параметра `api-version` |
712. | `VertexAiProviderTest` | используется `FakeGoogleCredentials` (встроенный mock) |
713. | `OllamaProviderTest` | локальный Docker‑контейнер `ollama/ollama` запускается в CI (порт 11434) |
714. | `LlamaCppProviderTest` | `ProcessBuilder`‑мок (используем `MockProcess`) |
715. | `ProviderRegistryTest` | загрузка из `llm_providers.yaml` и динамический JAR‑плагин |
716. | `SettingsViewModelTest` | включение/выключение, сохранение в `EncryptedSharedPreferences` |
717. 
718. Все новые тесты добавляются в `agent-core/src/test/kotlin/ru/agentshell/llm/provider/` и участвуют в уже существующем CI‑pipeline (`./gradlew test`).  
719. 
720. ---
721. 
722. ## 9️⃣ CI‑pipeline (GitHub Actions)
723. 
724. ```yaml
725. jobs:
726.   build:
727.     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
728.     services:
729.       ollama:
730.         image: ollama/ollama
731.         ports: ["11434:11434"]
732.     steps:
733.       - uses: actions/checkout@v4
734.       - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
735.         with:
736.           distribution: 'temurin'
737.           java-version: '21'
738.       - name: Cache Gradle
739.         uses: actions/cache@v3
740.         with:
741.           path: ~/.gradle/caches
742.           key: ${{ runner.os }}-gradle-${{ hashFiles('**/*.gradle*') }}
743.       - name: Run unit tests (core)
744.         env:
745.           OPENAI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.OPENAI_API_KEY }}
746.           ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
747.           AZURE_OPENAI_KEY: ${{ secrets.AZURE_OPENAI_KEY }}
748.           # (добавить остальные)
749.         run: ./gradlew :agent-core:test
750. ```
751. 
752. *Тесты, требующие реального облака, запускаются только если переменные окружения заданы; иначе тесты `assumeTrue` их пропускают.*

data class SandboxPolicy(

    val cpuTimeMs: Long,
    val wallTimeMs: Long,

    val outputBytes: Long,

✔ #118 bash - Check ProotSandbox implementation 40m 39s
$ cd /root/AgentShell && cat android-app/src/main/kotlin/com/agentshell/app/service/ProotSandbox.kt
package com.agentshell.app.service

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File

/**
 * Manages the Proot-based Alpine Linux sandbox.
 *
 * Prerequisites:
 *  - assets/rootfs.tar.gz  (built externally with build_rootfs.sh)
 *  - jniLibs/{abi}/libproot.so  (prebuilt proot binaries)
 */
class ProotSandbox(private val context: Context) {

    private var process: Process? = null
    private val rootfsDir get() = File(context.filesDir, "prootfs")
    private val prootBin get() = File(context.applicationInfo.nativeLibraryDir, "libproot.so")

    val isRunning: Boolean get() = process?.isAlive == true

    /** Extract rootfs from assets if not already done, then start proot. */
    suspend fun start(): Result<Unit> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        runCatching {
            if (!rootfsDir.exists()) extractRootfs()
            launchProot()
        }
    }

    private fun extractRootfs() {
        Log.i(TAG, "Extracting Alpine rootfs…")
        rootfsDir.mkdirs()
        context.assets.open("rootfs.tar.gz").use { assetStream ->
            val tarGz = File(context.cacheDir, "rootfs.tar.gz")
            tarGz.outputStream().use { assetStream.copyTo(it) }
            // Use system tar to extract (available on Android 8+)
            Runtime.getRuntime().exec(
                arrayOf("tar", "-xzf", tarGz.absolutePath, "-C", rootfsDir.absolutePath)
            ).waitFor()
            tarGz.delete()
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "Rootfs extracted to ${rootfsDir.absolutePath}")
    }

    private fun launchProot() {
        if (!prootBin.exists()) error("libproot.so not found at ${prootBin.absolutePath}")
        val cmd = arrayOf(
            prootBin.absolutePath,
            "--rootfs=${rootfsDir.absolutePath}",
            "--bind=/dev", "--bind=/proc", "--bind=/sys",
            "--change-id=0:0",
            "/bin/sh", "-c", "exec /bin/sh"
        )
        process = ProcessBuilder(*cmd)
            .redirectErrorStream(true)
            .directory(rootfsDir)
            .start()
        Log.i(TAG, "Proot sandbox started (PID not directly accessible via ProcessBuilder)")
    }

    /** Send a shell command to the running sandbox. Returns stdout. */
    suspend fun exec(command: String): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val p = process ?: error("Sandbox not running")
        p.outputStream.write("$command\n".toByteArray())
        p.outputStream.flush()
        // For real streaming output use McpChannel or a dedicated reader thread
        ""
    }

    fun stop() {
        process?.destroy()
        process = null
        Log.i(TAG, "Proot sandbox stopped")
    }

    companion object { private const val TAG = "ProotSandbox" }
}

✔ #122 bash - Check McpChannel and TermuxConnector 40m 59s
$ cd /root/AgentShell && cat android-app/src/main/kotlin/com/agentshell/app/service/McpChannel.kt | head -60 && echo "---" && cat android-app/src/main/kotlin/com/agentshell/app/service/TermuxConnector.kt | head -50
package com.agentshell.app.service

import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.encodeToString
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.io.OutputStream

@Serializable
data class AgentMessage(
    val type: String,
    val runId: String? = null,
    val payload: String? = null
)

class McpChannel(
    private val input: InputStream,
    private val output: OutputStream
) {
    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }
    private val _incoming = MutableSharedFlow<AgentMessage>(extraBufferCapacity = 100)
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)

    init { startReading() }

    private fun startReading() = scope.launch {
        val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(input))
        var line: String?
        while (reader.readLine().also { line = it } != null) {
            line?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let {
                runCatching { json.decodeFromString<AgentMessage>(it) }
                    .onSuccess { msg -> _incoming.tryEmit(msg) }
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun send(msg: AgentMessage) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val text = json.encodeToString(msg) + "\n"
        output.write(text.toByteArray())
        output.flush()
    }

    fun receive(): Flow<AgentMessage> = _incoming.asSharedFlow()

    fun close() {
        scope.cancel()
        runCatching { input.close() }
        runCatching { output.close() }
    }
}

import android.content.Context

private const val TERMUX_PACKAGE = "com.termux"
private const val SOCKET_NAME = "agentshell_socket"

class TermuxConnector(private val context: Context) {

    private var socket: LocalSocket? = null

    val isTermuxInstalled: Boolean
        get() = runCatching {
            context.packageManager.getPackageInfo(TERMUX_PACKAGE, 0)
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)

    /** Copy agent-core JAR and startup script to Termux home, then launch server. */
    suspend fun install() = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val termuxHome = File("/data/data/$TERMUX_PACKAGE/files/home/agentshell")
        termuxHome.mkdirs()

        // Copy server script from assets
        context.assets.open("termux/agent_shell_server.sh").use { inp ->
            File(termuxHome, "agent_shell_server.sh").outputStream().use { inp.copyTo(it) }
        }
        File(termuxHome, "agent_shell_server.sh").setExecutable(true)
        Log.i(TAG, "Installed agent_shell_server.sh to $termuxHome")
    }

    /** Start the agent server inside Termux via am broadcast. */
    fun startServer() {
        val intent = Intent("com.termux.app.RUN_COMMAND").apply {
            setPackage(TERMUX_PACKAGE)
            putExtra("com.termux.app.RUN_COMMAND_PATH", "/data/data/$TERMUX_PACKAGE/files/home/agentshell/agent_shell_server.sh")
            putExtra("com.termux.app.RUN_COMMAND_BACKGROUND", true)
        }
        context.sendBroadcast(intent)
        Log.i(TAG, "Sent RUN_COMMAND to Termux")
    }

    /** Connect to the Unix-domain socket exposed by the agent server. */

package com.agentshell.app.service

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Binder
import android.os.IBinder
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.agentshell.app.AgentShellApp
import com.agentshell.app.R

import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow

private const val TAG = "AgentRuntimeService"
private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1

class AgentRuntimeService : Service() {

    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)
    private val _events = MutableSharedFlow<AgentMessage>(extraBufferCapacity = 200)

    private var prootSandbox: ProotSandbox? = null
    private var termuxConnector: TermuxConnector? = null
    private var mcpChannel: McpChannel? = null

    inner class LocalBinder : Binder() {
        val service get() = this@AgentRuntimeService
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder = LocalBinder()

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification("Agent runtime starting…"))
        Log.i(TAG, "Service created")
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_START_EMBEDDED -> startEmbedded()
            ACTION_START_TERMUX -> startExternal()
            ACTION_STOP -> stopSelf()
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startEmbedded() {
        val sandbox = ProotSandbox(this).also { prootSandbox = it }
        scope.launch {
            sandbox.start()
                .onSuccess {
                    val ch = McpChannel(sandbox.process!!.inputStream, sandbox.process!!.outputStream)
                    mcpChannel = ch
                    forwardEvents(ch)
                    updateNotification("Alpine sandbox running")
                }
                .onFailure { Log.e(TAG, "Failed to start sandbox", it) }
        }
    }

    private fun startExternal() {
        val connector = TermuxConnector(this).also { termuxConnector = it }
        scope.launch {
            runCatching {
                connector.install()
                connector.startServer()
                delay(2_000) // wait for server to start
                val ch = connector.connect()
                mcpChannel = ch
                forwardEvents(ch)
                updateNotification("Termux agent running")
            }.onFailure { Log.e(TAG, "Termux connection failed", it) }
        }
    }

$ cd /root/AgentShell && cat agent-core/src/main/kotlin/dev/agentshell/executor/SandboxGuard.kt && echo "===" && cat agent-core/src/main/kotlin/dev/agentshell/executor/ShellToolExecutor.kt | head -60
package dev.agentshell.executor

import dev.agentshell.domain.ErrorCode
import dev.agentshell.domain.SandboxPolicy
import dev.agentshell.domain.ToolCall
import dev.agentshell.domain.ToolResult

/**
 * Enforces SandboxPolicy constraints before and after tool execution.
 */
object SandboxGuard {

    /**
     * Checks whether [path] is permitted by [policy]. Returns an error ToolResult if not.
     */
    fun checkPath(call: ToolCall, policy: SandboxPolicy, path: String): ToolResult? {
        if (policy.allowedPaths.isEmpty()) return null
        val resolved = java.nio.file.Paths.get(path).normalize().toAbsolutePath()
        val allowed = policy.allowedPaths.any { pattern ->
            val base = expandEnvVars(pattern.removeSuffix("/**").removeSuffix("/*"))
            resolved.startsWith(java.nio.file.Paths.get(base).normalize().toAbsolutePath())
        }
        if (!allowed) {
            return ToolResult(
                callId = call.callId,
                success = false,
                errorCode = ErrorCode.ERR_PATH_VIOLATION,
                errorDetail = "Path '$path' not in allowed sandbox paths: ${policy.allowedPaths}",
            )
        }
        return null
    }

    /**
     * Checks whether network access to [host] is permitted by [policy]. Returns an error ToolResult if not.
     */
    fun checkNetwork(call: ToolCall, policy: SandboxPolicy, host: String): ToolResult? {
        if (!policy.network) {
            return ToolResult(
                callId = call.callId,
                success = false,
                errorCode = ErrorCode.ERR_SANDBOX_KILLED,
                errorDetail = "Network access is disabled by sandbox policy '${policy.name}'",
            )
        }
        if (policy.allowedHosts.isNotEmpty() && !policy.allowedHosts.any { host.endsWith(it) }) {
            return ToolResult(
                callId = call.callId,
                success = false,
                errorCode = ErrorCode.ERR_SANDBOX_KILLED,
                errorDetail = "Host '$host' is not in allowed hosts: ${policy.allowedHosts}",
            )
        }
        return null
    }

    /** Truncates output to policy.outputBytes limit. */
    fun limitOutput(output: String, policy: SandboxPolicy): String {
        val bytes = output.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        if (bytes.size <= policy.outputBytes) return output
        val truncated = String(bytes, 0, policy.outputBytes.toInt(), Charsets.UTF_8)
        return "$truncated\n[OUTPUT TRUNCATED at ${policy.outputBytes} bytes]"
    }

    fun expandEnvVars(path: String): String {
        return path
            .replace("\$REPO_ROOT", System.getProperty("agentshell.repoRoot") ?: System.getProperty("user.dir") ?: ".")
            .replace("\$HOME", System.getProperty("user.home") ?: "~")
    }
}
===
package dev.agentshell.executor

import dev.agentshell.domain.ErrorCode
import dev.agentshell.domain.SandboxPolicy
import dev.agentshell.domain.ToolCall
import dev.agentshell.domain.ToolContract
import dev.agentshell.domain.ToolResult
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonObject
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonPrimitive
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * Executes shell commands.
 * Expected args: { "command": "...", "workingDir": "..." (optional) }
 */
class ShellToolExecutor : ToolExecutor {

    override val toolName = "shell_exec"

    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }

    override fun execute(call: ToolCall, contract: ToolContract, policy: SandboxPolicy): ToolResult {
        val args = json.parseToJsonElement(call.argumentsJson).jsonObject
        val command = args["command"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content
            ?: return ToolResult(
                callId = call.callId,
                success = false,
                errorCode = ErrorCode.ERR_SCHEMA_VIOLATION,
                errorDetail = "Missing required field: command",
            )

        val workingDir = args["workingDir"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content
            ?: System.getProperty("user.dir")

        SandboxGuard.checkPath(call, policy, workingDir)?.let { return it }

        return runProcess(call, policy, listOf("sh", "-c", command), File(workingDir))
    }

    private fun runProcess(
        call: ToolCall,
        policy: SandboxPolicy,
        cmd: List<String>,
        workDir: File,
    ): ToolResult {
        return try {
            val process = ProcessBuilder(cmd)
                .directory(workDir)

package dev.agentshell.llm.provider

import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmTool
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmToolCall
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.emptyFlow

data class CompletionRequest(
    val messages: List<LlmMessage>,
    val tools: List<LlmTool> = emptyList(),
    val systemPrompt: String? = null,
    val maxTokens: Int = 4096,
    val temperature: Double = 0.7,
    val modelOverride: String? = null,
)

data class CompletionResponse(
    val content: String?,
    val toolCalls: List<LlmToolCall> = emptyList(),
    val stopReason: LlmResponse.StopReason = LlmResponse.StopReason.END_TURN,
    val inputTokens: Int = 0,
    val outputTokens: Int = 0,
    val providerId: String = "",
    val modelUsed: String = "",
) {
    fun toLlmResponse() = LlmResponse(
        content = content,
        toolCalls = toolCalls,
        stopReason = stopReason,
        inputTokens = inputTokens,
        outputTokens = outputTokens,
    )
}

data class CompletionChunk(val delta: String, val done: Boolean = false)

interface LlmProvider {
    val id: String
    val displayName: String
    val isLocal: Boolean get() = false

    suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse

    fun stream(request: CompletionRequest): Flow<CompletionChunk> = emptyFlow()
}
===
package dev.agentshell.llm.provider

import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

object LlmProviderRegistry {
    private val providers = ConcurrentHashMap<String, LlmProvider>()

    fun register(provider: LlmProvider) {
        providers[provider.id] = provider
    }

    fun get(id: String): LlmProvider? = providers[id]

    fun all(): List<LlmProvider> = providers.values.sortedBy { it.id }

    fun enabled(): List<LlmProvider> = all() // all registered = enabled

    fun clear() = providers.clear()
}
===
package dev.agentshell.llm.provider

import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory

class LlmException(message: String, cause: Throwable? = null) : Exception(message, cause)

class MultiProviderRouter(
    private val registry: LlmProviderRegistry = LlmProviderRegistry,
    val fallbackOrder: List<String> = listOf("ollama", "openai", "groq", "mistral"),
) {
    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MultiProviderRouter::class.java)

    suspend fun complete(request: CompletionRequest): CompletionResponse {
        val order = if (fallbackOrder.isEmpty()) registry.all().map { it.id } else fallbackOrder
        var lastError: Exception? = null
        for (id in order) {
            val provider = registry.get(id) ?: continue
            try {
                log.debug("Trying provider: {}", id)
                val resp = provider.complete(request)
                log.debug("Provider {} succeeded", id)
                return resp
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                log.warn("Provider {} failed: {}", id, e.message)
                lastError = e
            }
        }
        throw LlmException("All fallback providers failed. Last error: ${lastError?.message}", lastError)
    }
}

✔ #130 bash - Check BaseHttpProvider 43m 28s
$ cd /root/AgentShell && cat agent-core/src/main/kotlin/dev/agentshell/llm/provider/impl/BaseHttpProvider.kt
package dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl

import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmTool
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmToolCall
import dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionRequest
import dev.agentshell.llm.provider.CompletionResponse
import dev.agentshell.llm.provider.LlmException
import dev.agentshell.llm.provider.LlmProvider
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.serialization.json.*
import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory

abstract class BaseHttpProvider(protected val http: OkHttpClient) : LlmProvider {
    protected val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(this::class.java)
    protected val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }
    protected val JSON_MT = "application/json".toMediaType()

    protected suspend fun post(url: String, body: JsonObject, headers: Map<String, String>): String =
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val reqBody = json.encodeToString(JsonObject.serializer(), body).toRequestBody(JSON_MT)
            val reqBuilder = Request.Builder().url(url).post(reqBody)
            headers.forEach { (k, v) -> reqBuilder.header(k, v) }
            http.newCall(reqBuilder.build()).execute().use { resp ->
                val bodyStr = resp.body?.string() ?: ""
                if (!resp.isSuccessful) {
                    log.error("{} HTTP {}: {}", id, resp.code, bodyStr.take(300))
                    throw LlmException("$id HTTP ${resp.code}: ${bodyStr.take(200)}")
                }
                bodyStr
            }
        }

    /** Build messages array in OpenAI format */
    protected fun buildMessagesArray(messages: List<LlmMessage>, systemPrompt: String?): JsonArray = buildJsonArray {
        systemPrompt?.let { add(buildJsonObject { put("role", "system"); put("content", it) }) }
        for (msg in messages) {
            add(buildJsonObject {
                put("role", msg.role.name)
                msg.toolCallId?.let { put("tool_call_id", it) }
                msg.content?.let { put("content", it) }
                if (msg.toolCalls.isNotEmpty()) {
                    put("tool_calls", buildJsonArray {
                        msg.toolCalls.forEach { tc ->
                            add(buildJsonObject {
                                put("id", tc.id)
                                put("type", "function")
                                put("function", buildJsonObject {
                                    put("name", tc.toolName)
                                    put("arguments", tc.argumentsJson)
                                })
                            })
                        }
                    })
                }
            })
        }
    }

    protected fun buildToolsArray(tools: List<LlmTool>): JsonArray = buildJsonArray {
        tools.forEach { tool ->
            add(buildJsonObject {
                put("type", "function")
                put("function", buildJsonObject {
                    put("name", tool.name)
                    put("description", tool.description)
                    put("parameters", json.parseToJsonElement(tool.parametersJson))
                })
            })
        }
    }

    /** Parse OpenAI-compatible response */
    protected fun parseOpenAiResponse(body: String, provId: String, modelName: String): CompletionResponse {
        val root = json.parseToJsonElement(body).jsonObject
        val usage = root["usage"]?.jsonObject
        val choice = root["choices"]?.jsonArray?.firstOrNull()?.jsonObject
            ?: return CompletionResponse(content = null, providerId = provId, modelUsed = modelName)
        val message = choice["message"]?.jsonObject
            ?: return CompletionResponse(content = null, providerId = provId, modelUsed = modelName)
        val content = message["content"]?.jsonPrimitive?.contentOrNull
        val finishReason = choice["finish_reason"]?.jsonPrimitive?.contentOrNull
        val toolCalls = message["tool_calls"]?.jsonArray?.map { tc ->
            val o = tc.jsonObject
            val fn = o["function"]?.jsonObject
            LlmToolCall(
                id = o["id"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content ?: "",
                toolName = fn?.get("name")?.jsonPrimitive?.content ?: "",
                argumentsJson = fn?.get("arguments")?.jsonPrimitive?.content ?: "{}",
            )
        } ?: emptyList()
        val stopReason = when {
            toolCalls.isNotEmpty() -> dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse.StopReason.TOOL_USE
            finishReason == "length" -> dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse.StopReason.MAX_TOKENS
            else -> dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse.StopReason.END_TURN
        }
        return CompletionResponse(
            content = content,
            toolCalls = toolCalls,
            stopReason = stopReason,
            inputTokens = usage?.get("prompt_tokens")?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0,
            outputTokens = usage?.get("completion_tokens")?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull ?: 0,
            providerId = provId,
            modelUsed = modelName,
        )
    }
}

package dev.agentshell.llm.provider

import com.charleskorn.kaml.Yaml
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory

@Serializable
data class ProviderYaml(
    val id: String = "",
    val enabled: Boolean = false,
    val apiKey: String? = null,
    val model: String? = null,
    val host: String? = null,
    val endpoint: String? = null,
    val deployment: String? = null,
    val projectId: String? = null,
    val location: String? = null,
    val modelPath: String? = null,
    val binaryPath: String? = null,
    val threads: Int = 4,
    val maxTokens: Int = 4096,
    val temperature: Double = 0.7,
)

@Serializable
data class LlmProvidersConfig(
    val providers: List<ProviderYaml> = emptyList(),
)

object LlmProvidersLoader {
    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(LlmProvidersLoader::class.java)

    fun load(resourcePath: String = "llm_providers.yaml"): LlmProvidersConfig {
        val stream = Thread.currentThread().contextClassLoader
            ?.getResourceAsStream(resourcePath)
            ?: LlmProvidersLoader::class.java.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream(resourcePath)
            ?: run {
                log.warn("llm_providers.yaml not found, using empty config")
                return LlmProvidersConfig()
            }
        return try {
            val text = stream.bufferedReader().readText()
                .replace(Regex("\\$\\{([^}]+)\\}")) { mr ->
                    System.getenv(mr.groupValues[1]) ?: ""
                }
            Yaml.default.decodeFromString(LlmProvidersConfig.serializer(), text)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            log.error("Failed to load llm_providers.yaml: {}", e.message)
            LlmProvidersConfig()
        }
    }

    fun registerAll(config: LlmProvidersConfig) {
        val okHttpClient = okhttp3.OkHttpClient.Builder()
            .callTimeout(60, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .connectTimeout(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .build()

        config.providers.filter { it.enabled }.forEach { p ->
            val provider: LlmProvider? = when (p.id) {
                "openai" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.OpenAiProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "anthropic" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.AnthropicProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "azure_openai" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.AzureOpenAiProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "vertex_ai" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.VertexAiProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "ollama" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.OllamaProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "huggingface" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.HuggingFaceProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "cohere" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.CohereProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "mistral" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.MistralProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "groq" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.GroqProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "deepseek" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.DeepSeekProvider(okHttpClient, p)
                "llama_cpp" -> dev.agentshell.llm.provider.impl.LlamaCppProvider(p)
                else -> { log.warn("Unknown provider id: {}", p.id); null }
            }
            provider?.let { LlmProviderRegistry.register(it) }
        }
        log.info("Registered {} LLM providers", LlmProviderRegistry.all().size)
    }
}

$ cd /root/AgentShell && cat agent-core/src/main/kotlin/dev/agentshell/security/SecretsVault.kt | head -40 && echo "===" && cat agent-core/src/main/kotlin/dev/agentshell/runtime/WatchdogService.kt | head -40
package dev.agentshell.security

import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/**
 * Thread-safe registry of secret values that must be masked in logs and audit events.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   SecretsVault.register("OPENAI_KEY", System.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"))
 *   SecretsVault.mask("Bearer sk-abc123...") // → "Bearer [REDACTED]"
 *
 * Automatically called by [registerLlmKeys] to mask all known LLM API keys.
 */
object SecretsVault {

    private val secrets = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()
    private const val REDACTED = "[REDACTED]"

    /** Register a secret value to be masked everywhere it appears. */
    fun register(value: String) {
        if (value.isNotBlank() && value.length > 4) {
            secrets.addIfAbsent(value)
        }
    }

    /** Register a named secret from an environment variable (no-op if env var is unset). */
    fun registerEnv(envVar: String) {
        System.getenv(envVar)?.let { register(it) }
    }

    /** Register all known LLM API key environment variables. */
    fun registerLlmKeys() {
        registerEnv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
        registerEnv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
        registerEnv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        registerEnv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
        registerEnv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")
    }

    /** Replace all registered secrets in the given string with [REDACTED]. */

/**
 * Detects stuck runs by periodically comparing heartbeat timestamps against
 * [stuckThresholdMs]. A run is considered stuck if it is RUNNING but its
 * heartbeat has not been updated within the threshold.
 *
 * On detecting a stuck run: marks it CRASHED + records a RUN_CRASHED audit event.
 */
class WatchdogService(
    private val stateStore: StateStore,
    private val stuckThresholdMs: Long = DEFAULT_STUCK_THRESHOLD_MS,
    private val checkIntervalMs: Long = DEFAULT_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS,
    private val auditTrail: AuditTrail? = null,
    private val clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC(),
) {
    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(WatchdogService::class.java)
    private var scheduler: ScheduledExecutorService? = null

    fun start() {
        scheduler = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor { r ->
            Thread(r, "agentshell-watchdog").also { it.isDaemon = true }
        }
        scheduler!!.scheduleAtFixedRate(::check, checkIntervalMs, checkIntervalMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
        log.info("WatchdogService started: checkInterval={}ms stuckThreshold={}ms", checkIntervalMs, stuckThresholdMs)
    }

    fun stop() {

package dev.agentshell.orchestrator

import dev.agentshell.audit.AuditTrail
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmGateway
import dev.agentshell.memory.MemoryAugmentedGateway
import dev.agentshell.memory.MemoryIndexer

import dev.agentshell.runtime.AgentRunner
import dev.agentshell.state.StateStore
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.Future

/**
 * Orchestrates multiple agents defined in an OrchestratorPlan.
 *
 * - Resolves execution order via topological sort (respects dependsOn)
 * - Runs agents within a wave in parallel on a thread pool
 * - Injects upstream agent results into downstream agent goals as [UPSTREAM_RESULT]
 * - Optionally integrates with MemoryStore for cross-agent memory
 */
class Orchestrator(
    private val stateStore: StateStore,
    private val gatewayFactory: (AgentSpec) -> LlmGateway?,
    private val auditTrailFactory: ((String) -> AuditTrail)? = null,
    private val memoryStore: MemoryStore? = null,
    private val parallelism: Int = 4
) {

    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(Orchestrator::class.java)
    private val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(parallelism)

    data class AgentResult(
        val agentId: String,
        val runId: String,
        val status: String,
        val summary: String?
    )

    data class OrchestrationResult(
        val planName: String,
        val agentResults: List<AgentResult>,
        val success: Boolean
    )

    fun run(plan: OrchestratorPlan): OrchestrationResult {
        log.info("Starting orchestration plan '${plan.name}' with ${plan.agents.size} agents")
        val waves = OrchestratorPlanLoader.topologicalSort(plan)
        val allResults = mutableListOf<AgentResult>()
        val resultsByAgent = mutableMapOf<String, AgentResult>()

package dev.agentshell.memory

import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmGateway
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmMessage
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmResponse
import dev.agentshell.llm.LlmTool

/**
 * Wraps any LlmGateway and prepends relevant past-run memories into
 * the system prompt before each completion request.
 *
 * Retrieves top-K memories by TF-IDF cosine similarity to the user's last message.
 */
class MemoryAugmentedGateway(
    private val delegate: LlmGateway,
    private val memoryStore: MemoryStore,
    private val topK: Int = 3
) : LlmGateway {

    override fun complete(
        messages: List<LlmMessage>,
        tools: List<LlmTool>,
        systemPrompt: String?
    ): LlmResponse {
        val query = messages.lastOrNull { it.role == LlmMessage.Role.user }?.content ?: ""
        val memories = memoryStore.search(query, topK)

        val augmentedSystem = if (memories.isEmpty()) systemPrompt
        else buildAugmentedPrompt(systemPrompt, memories)

        return delegate.complete(messages, tools, augmentedSystem)
    }

    private fun buildAugmentedPrompt(base: String?, memories: List<MemoryEntry>): String {
        val memoryBlock = buildString {
            appendLine("=== Relevant past experiences ===")
            memories.forEachIndexed { i, m ->
                appendLine("[Memory ${i + 1}] run=${m.runId.take(8)} agent=${m.agentId} score=${"%.2f".format(m.score)}")
                appendLine(m.text)
                if (i < memories.lastIndex) appendLine("---")
